# Third-party licenses

IRIXMAIL 0.1.3 bundles the dependencies listed below inside its binary.
IRIXMAIL itself is licensed AGPL-3.0-only; see LICENSE.

Regenerate with `bash scripts/third-party-licenses.sh`.

## Rust dependencies
